Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any dynamics module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 62. The projective resolution has length at most 245 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 62 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the dynamics structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 245. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on dynamics structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 317. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 112 with relations in degree 1.
